```solidity
// ?좏뻾 怨쇱젙 ?섏? ERC-20
contract MyToken {
    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;
    uint256 public totalSupply;
    address public owner;

    constructor() { owner = msg.sender; }

    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {
        require(msg.sender == owner, "not owner");
        balanceOf[to] += amount;
        totalSupply += amount;
    }

    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external {
        require(balanceOf[msg.sender] >= amount, "insufficient");
        balanceOf[msg.sender] -= amount;
        balanceOf[to] += amount;
    }
}
```

```solidity
contract Logic {
    uint256 public number;  // slot 0
    
    function setNumber(uint256 x) external {
        number = x;
    }
}

contract Caller {
    uint256 public number;  // slot 0
    address public logic;
    
    // ?? call 踰꾩쟾 ??
    function callSet(uint256 x) external {
        logic.call(
            abi.encodeWithSignature("setNumber(uint256)", x)
        );
        // 寃곌낵: Logic.number 媛 x 濡?諛붾?
        //       Caller.number ??洹몃?濡?0
    }
    
    // ?? delegatecall 踰꾩쟾 ??
    function delegateSet(uint256 x) external {
        logic.delegatecall(
            abi.encodeWithSignature("setNumber(uint256)", x)
        );
        // 寃곌낵: Caller.number 媛 x 濡?諛붾?
        //       Logic.number ??洹몃?濡?0
    }
}
```

```solidity
bytes32 private constant _IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT = 
    0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc;

function _getImplementation() internal view returns (address impl) {
    assembly {
        impl := sload(_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT)
    }
}

function _setImplementation(address newImpl) internal {
    assembly {
        sstore(_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT, newImpl)
    }
}
```

`InternalGatewayClient`媛 ?대? RPC瑜?泥섎━?섎뒗 諛⑹떇:

```typescript
// internal/packages/core-banking/src/adapters/InternalGatewayClient.ts

export class InternalGatewayClient {
  constructor(private config: {
    baseUrl: string;  // "http://internal-ledger:8080"
    secret: string;   // INTERNAL_GATEWAY_SECRET ?섍꼍蹂??
  }) {}

  private async request<T>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<T> {
    const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}${path}`, {
      method,
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'X-Internal-Secret': this.secret,   // ?대?留??몄쬆
      },
      body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new InternalGatewayError(method, path, res.status, ...);
    return res.json();
  }

  // Java DTO? 1:1 留ㅽ븨
  async recordNftHolding(userId: string, req: GatewayNftHoldingRequest): Promise<void> {
    await this.request('POST', `/api/internal/users/${userId}/nft-holdings`, req);
  }

  async recordAuditLog(req: GatewayAuditLogRequest): Promise<void> {
    await this.request('POST', '/api/internal/audit-log', req);
  }
}
```
